- **migration-safety**: Stops destructive DB work from running before the plan exists. A PreToolUse hook blocks destructive SQL — DROP TABLE/COLUMN, TRUNCATE, DELETE/UPDATE without WHERE, irreversible ALTERs — in Bash commands or migration-file writes unless a migration plan is declared (.migration-plan file, or MIGRATION-SAFETY: ALLOW sentinel stating target, dry-run, rollback). The skill teaches the method: state target → dry-run → rollback plan → then execute. WHERE-scoped deletes, additive migrations, and SQL in docs pass; fails open. Stops 'clean up the old rows → forget the WHERE → gone' from ever running. ([logicleap-labs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/logicleap-labs/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/migration-safety) · ★2 · 1 skill, 1 command, 1 hook file)
